If it finds one, the kit goes back to the queue with its retry count reset. (2) Why sle_stm_v1 clbe630b9c (E8, QC-ALLFIX 1124052610) has no report: the kit did reach GRT. 5_1_grt.odb and 5_global_route.rpt exist, overflow H/V is 0, and the GRT report gives wns 0.00 and worst slack +3.13. ORFS writes the congestion marker report only when GRT is congested, so a clean GRT leaves none, and my judge had wrongly required one. Fixed: a GRT DB with 0 overflow and the GRT-report wns >= -42 now counts as a clean GRT. sle_stm_v1 cl27746 re-judges on the same rule. QC-ALLFIX: the kit ended route FAIL on its own chain stop (deepest grt failing SETUP_TRIAGE,drv,final, exit 6), not at GRT congestion. Also, the census STA read only 4_cts_post_hook.rpt (worst -32.9 u_core.u_stream->u_bout_flt_o, CLOCK-SKEW).

(1) 0 paths drawn: every SMELL/checkpoint render (noprog_watchdog.smell_render -> render_review.sh) now runs tools/autopilot/stage_paths.tcl on the rendered DB itself. It loads the kit libraries and the stage SDC (never a later stage's SDC), uses ideal clocks before CTS and propagated from 4_cts, and global_routing parasitics on GRT/route DBs. It writes the worst-50 setup and worst-50 hold paths; render_review.py draws them (met paths included) when there is no sign-off STA, and summary.txt says "paths drawn N of M" with worst setup/hold slack. Smoke on QC coll_ftn (epyc8): 3_3_place_gp 100/100 drawn (setup worst -130 ps, 4/50 negative; hold at ideal clock labelled as before hold repair), 5_1_grt 100/100 drawn (setup +35 ps, hold -116 ps 2/50). (2) Smells 'checkpoint only': the SMELL render now carries the kit's own layout_smells line from run/layout_review/<stage>/smells.one. 'Not computable' on big DBs (> 400k insts): layout_render.tcl now dumps sampled connectivity instead of none. Ports, macro pins, clock pins and repeater trees are kept complete; other nets 1 in k (OT_LR_SAMPLE_ROOTS, default 100k). opposite_face_pins, channel_tracks, clock and fanout_spread now compute, labelled SAMPLED (counts are lower bounds). New renders pick this up when the hosts' rt updates to 92824012+. Tests: test_autopilot stage_paths, test_noprog_smell render, test_layout_render sampled + lr_keep_full (tclsh mock); 241 pass across the 4 files.
